<script>
    $(document).ready(function() {
        var contentMenuCategory = $('[data-content-menu-category]');
        var mobileMenuButton = $('[data-trigger-mobile-menu]');
        var mobileMenu = $('[data-mobile-menu]');
        var mobileMenuCategoryAccordionTrigger = $('[data-mobile-menu-accordion-item-trigger]');

        // change the aria-expanded label to reflect the visibility-state of the submenu in an accessible way in the content-navigation
        contentMenuCategory.hover(
            function() {
                var dropdownLinkTrigger = $(this).find('[aria-haspopup]');
                dropdownLinkTrigger.attr('aria-expanded', true);
            },
            function() {
                var dropdownLinkTrigger = $(this).find('[aria-haspopup]');
                dropdownLinkTrigger.attr('aria-expanded', false);
            })

        // show and hide the mobile menu via click on the burger/close button
        mobileMenuButton.on('click', function() {
            var parent = $(this).parent();

            if (mobileMenu.hasClass('show')) {
                $(this).attr('aria-expanded', false);
                $('html').removeClass('noscroll');
                mobileMenu.removeClass('show');
                parent.removeClass('is-showing-close');
                parent.addClass('is-showing-burger');
            } else {
                $(this).attr('aria-expanded', true);
                mobileMenu.addClass('show');
                $('html').addClass('noscroll');
                parent.removeClass('is-showing-burger');
                parent.addClass('is-showing-close');
            }
        });

        // accordion inside the mobile menu
        mobileMenuCategoryAccordionTrigger.on('click', function() {
            var parentTab = $(this).closest('[data-mobile-menu-accordion-item]');
            var otherTabs = $('[data-mobile-menu-accordion-item]');
            var tabContent = parentTab.find('[data-mobile-menu-accordion-item-content]');
            var tabContentHeightWrapper = tabContent.find('[data-content-menu-category-submenu]');

            if (parentTab.hasClass('is-expanded')) {
                // close it
                tabContent.removeAttr('style');
                parentTab.removeClass('is-expanded');
                parentTab.attr('aria-expanded', false);
            } else {
                var newHeight = tabContentHeightWrapper.outerHeight();

                // close all the other tabs first
                otherTabs.each(function() {
                    var otherTabContent = $(this).find('[data-mobile-menu-accordion-item-content]');
                    otherTabContent.removeAttr('style');
                    $(this).removeClass('is-expanded');
                    $(this).attr('aria-expanded', false);
                });

                // expand it
                tabContent.css('height', newHeight);
                parentTab.addClass('is-expanded');
                parentTab.attr('aria-expanded', true);
            }
        })
    });
</script> <div class="contentbox">

<script>
    // IIFE, to fake document.ready
    "use strict";

(function () {
  var stars = document.querySelectorAll('[data-animation-star-spinners]');
  var orbits = document.querySelectorAll('[data-animation-orbit]');
  var scene = document.querySelector('[data-animation-rocket-launch]');
  var animationEndEvent = 'webkitAnimationEnd mozAnimationEnd animationend'; // each star should spin on hover

  stars.forEach(function (star) {
    star.addEventListener('mouseover', function () {
      return star.classList.add('spin');
    }); // the class needs to be removed when the animation ends, so it can be retriggered again

    star.addEventListener('animationend', function (e) {
      return e.target.classList.remove('spin');
    });
  }); // this plays an animation that actually launches the rocket!

  document.querySelector('[data-animation-launch-button]').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var self = e.target;
    self.classList.toggle('clicked');
    scene.classList.toggle('launch-rocket');
    setTimeout(function () {
      scene.classList.toggle('launch-rocket');
      self.classList.toggle('clicked');
    }, 5000);
  }); // when the user clicks the cog, all orbits start spinning faster and faster, until they cannot spin faster, then they start with their original speed and speed up again when the user keeps clicking

  document.querySelector('[data-animation-speed-up-orbits]').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var self = e.target;
    var currentCogSpeed = parseInt(window.getComputedStyle(self).animationDuration);
    orbits.forEach(function (orbit) {
      var currentSpeed = parseInt(window.getComputedStyle(orbit).animationDuration);

      if (currentSpeed != 0) {
        orbit.style.animationDuration = currentSpeed / 2 + 's';
      } else {
        // this is the part that resets the orbits speed, so it can continue to speed up
        orbit.style.removeProperty('animation-duration');
      }
    });
  });
})();
</script> </div>

<script>
        $(document).ready(function(){
            $('#languageSelect ul a').on('click', function(e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                var lang = $(this).attr('data-lang');
                var url = $(this).attr('href');
                $.ajax(
                    'https://www.easyname.at/de/index/set-language-cookie/new-language/' + lang,
                    {
                        success : function (){
                            window.location.href = url;
                        }
                    }

                );
                return false;
            });
        });
    </script>
